Why I’m stopping here:
- QED is already strong: 0.7637
- SA is already low/good: 2.1218
- MW is safe: 404.85
- Compared with ligand 2, it has:
  - higher QED
  - lower SA
  - lower MW
  - acceptable polarity/lipophilicity balance

Rationale:
- The crossover product reduced desirability by removing the favorable anilide motif and increasing hydrophobic character.
- Restoring the p-tolyl anilide returned the molecule to the best-balanced scaffold among the explored options.
- Further edits risk worsening SA or QED without clear evidence of improving JNK3.

Final properties:
- QED = 0.7637
- SA = 2.1218
- Molecular weight = 404.85
- LogP = 4.4625
- TPSA = 66.48

So my proposed “better than current” answer is effectively to keep ligand 1 as the optimized choice from this short design sequence.
